## Inference Research Guidance Check
- Binary facts were separated from documentation evidence and source-shape inference. Addresses, bytes, offsets, xrefs, RTTI PMDs, instructions, stack frames, and literal strings are direct IDA facts; page relationships and prior names are documentation evidence; class/member/source-file spelling is a constrained inference.
- Existing assumptions were treated as fallible. In particular, no direct xref to `0x005007a0` was not accepted as no-code proof because two live NewUser inlines reproduce the constructor semantics and the retained body installs the exact class vptrs.
- The source reconstruction follows late-1999 through mid-2000s Visual C++ style: a compact derived class, ordinary virtual overrides, inline trivial constructor, inherited virtual destructor, integer frame fields, and compiler-regenerated RTTI/vtables/thunks.
- No finalized source uses `sub_`, `dword_`, `unk_`, raw vptr stores, deleting flags, or decompiler temporaries. Descriptive inferred names are chosen where original spelling is unavailable.
- Wave2/Wave3 references were ignored as stale and were not used as evidence.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation
- **Class base:** RTTI lists `DirectionButtonControlPane`, `ButtonControlPane`, `ControlPane`, `Pane`, `GrafPort`, `LObject`, `EventHandler`, and `TimerHandler`. The PMDs place the first six at offset zero, EventHandler at `+0xa0`, and TimerHandler at `+0xa4`. Best source inference is one direct public `ButtonControlPane` base; repeating inherited facets would change layout and vtable construction.
- **Field names/types:** raw constructor and virtual bodies use dwords at `+0x10c` and `+0x110`. The first multiplies by two to select a direction resource frame; the second is assigned `0/1` from visual state `11`. `int m_directionIndex` and `int m_stateFrameOffset` are the strongest human names/types.
- **Constructor liveness:** zero xrefs and no modeled function are real negative evidence, but two live inlined NewUser constructions initialize direction indices `0/1`, clear frame offset, and install the same three vptrs. The retained body is therefore an inline-capable source constructor copy, not dead data.
- **Constructor signature:** `retn 8`, first stack argument stored at `+0x10c`, and second passed to `ButtonControlPane` base construction. Existing `RectBounds` is exact size `0x10`; sibling Gender uses `const RectBounds &bounds`. Best signature is `DirectionButtonControlPane(int directionIndex, const RectBounds &bounds)`.
- **State override:** the byte argument is compared with inherited `m_visualState`; when changed, state `11` maps to frame offset `1` and all other states to `0`; base `ControlPane::SetVisualState` is always called. `SetVisualState(unsigned char)` is supported by the base/sibling naming and exact behavior.
- **Paint override:** the function creates `EPFTileContext`, copies inherited `m_visibleBounds`, selects `DIREC.EPD` frame `m_directionIndex * 2 + m_stateFrameOffset`, renders with palette `NPAL3.PAL`, then applies a color-143/draw-mode-2 fill overlay when disabled. `OnPaint()` is the correct virtual name.
- **Palette spelling and physical type:** in the dated report-time IDA snapshots, `NPAL3.PAL` was split into `aNp_1` plus `aAl3Pal`; exact 20 bytes decode one ten-wide-character null-terminated string. `aNpal3Pal` is the defensible IDA symbol and `L"NPAL3.PAL"` is the source literal. The supervisor's exact failed-unsaved I11 call proves that public `make_data` accepts the sized declaration to establish the 20-byte span but canonically returns and stores the physical type spelling `const wchar_t[]`. The array extent remains independently fixed by the item width (`20 / sizeof(wchar_t) = 10`) and exact bytes, so a second unproven `set_type` mutation is neither needed nor authorized. The split-item prestate is not permanent authority and must be dynamically reread during Gate 2B.
- **Destructor source:** the primary wrapper resets three vptrs, delegates to Pane teardown, and conditionally deletes; two thunks subtract `0xa0`/`0xa4`. These are MSVC ABI output. An explicit derived source destructor body is not evidenced; inherited virtual destruction is sufficient to regenerate them.
- **File placement:** code/address adjacency, existing Gender reconstruction, shared resource/render dependencies, and existing [UID:0000NY] routing support the shared `NexusTK/ui/controls/SpecializedButtonPanes.cpp/.h` bucket. A standalone `DirectionButtonControlPane.cpp` remains plausible but lacks stronger original filename evidence.
- **Rejected NewUser ownership:** NewUser contains consumers/inlined copies, not the reusable class's defining virtuals or RTTI owner.
- **Rejected direct vtable emission:** hand-authored arrays would preserve bytes only superficially while violating original source shape, compiler ABI ownership, inherited facets, and relocation behavior.
- **Rejected unchanged scores/blank source:** every prior blocker now has a concrete resolution or exact compiler-artifact disposition, so retaining `86/91` and blank class source would understate current evidence.

- The target's exact three-view layout and complete RTTI hierarchy are directly readable and internally consistent with class size `0x114`.

- The Gender sibling has already demonstrated the correct project topology: class H owns inline source/declarations, exact method children own out-of-line bodies, and compiler vtable/destructor records remain false/non-emitting.
- Resource and helper documentation now supports human names for every visible call in the proposed source; no compiler placeholder is needed in final C++.

## IDA Rename / Type / Comment Recommendations
All final actions in this section are supervisor-owned Gate 2B work. B004's authorized evidence is limited to bounded read-only MCP, exact bytes/layout/xrefs, documentation, and independent supervisor receipts. The unsaved noncanonical B004 I02 mutations were a prohibited role violation and provide no current evidence or authority. The action tables below therefore state only proposed actions, literal prestates, protected no-change identities, and expected readbacks; supervisor procedure governs runtime binding, database/session handling, backup, save, rollback, persistence, and process control externally.

void DirectionButtonControlPane::OnPaint()
